# Spec conformance

This ledger maps every claim the specification makes to the Foundry tests that pin it. The
specification is `Source/JaiScript/CLAUDE.md` ("Language features" and the rules around it), the
documents under `docs/` (diagnostics, static checking, reference model, namespaces, global
shadowing, char and integer semantics, exceptions, closures, parallel design, invariants, typed
regions and arrays, grammar), the user guide under `docs/site/guide/`, and C++ where those
documents say C++ semantics apply.

## The oracle

An expected value in a conformance test is derived from the specification by hand: the claim, the
C++ rule it names, or the arithmetic the claim implies. It is never taken from running a backend.
When the two backends disagree with the derived value, the backends are wrong; when the
specification is silent or contradicts itself, the claim is an open question (the last section)
and no test pins a reading of it.

## The suites

- `source/tests/semantics/spec_conformance_tests.cpp`, suite "Spec Conformance": one group per
  specification area (assignment values, declarations and constructors, returns and copies,
  memberwise equality, map keys, casts, catch variables, namespaces, utf8 caches, typed
  containers, increments, parallel locals, grammar and operators, chars, scopes and units,
  references, exceptions, closures, diagnostic texts, script the runtime calls, user `operator=`,
  error texts, checker verdicts, instance-variable overlays, file locations, privilege order,
  guide examples, parallel contracts, native exceptions, access control, and the rulings on
  lambda captures of globals, the order of a compound or subscript store, `auto`'s static type and
  call arguments, and the rulings of 2026-09-28).
- `source/tests/vm/spec_attack_tests.cpp`, suite "Spec Attacks": the VM's fast paths under
  pressure (raises inside cell blocks and grafts, coroutines over fast paths, host callbacks
  re-entering mid-loop, redefinition and hot reload mid-loop and mid-recursion, callees that
  shrink or clear a container under a loop, budget exhaustion inside a block, a debugger armed
  mid-run, parallel bodies with inlinable leaves, removed element aliases, and the compiler
  lowerings the execution census found no other test reaching).

Every row runs against one expected string on the interpreter and the VM, with type proofs on
and off, with the VM's IR passes off, and, in a build that carries the miss-injection lever
(`x64-Release MISS`), with injected cell misses every one and every three blocks. No group is
compiled out.

## Verdicts

- **pinned**: a registered test asserts the claim's specific behavior.
- **thin**: a test touches the feature but misses the exact edge, text, order or mode.
- **none**: no test.
- **contradicted**: registered tests pin the opposite of the document, or two documents disagree;
  the row names the tests that pin the other reading and the claim is listed as an open question.

The Fuzzer column says whether `jaiscript_fuzz` can generate the claim's shape (yes, partial, no);
a "no" row is covered by hand-written tests alone.

## Script the runtime calls

Every place the runtime runs script on the program's behalf, and what an error raised there does
(`docs/diagnostics.md`, "Errors in script the runtime calls"). "Own text" means the error leaves the
builtin or operator with the text it was raised with, catchable, and an uncaught one ends the program
with the callback's frames in the trace.

| Call-back | Contract | Pinned by |
|---|---|---|
| `sort(cmp)` comparator | own text or thrown value; the first failure ends the calls; the array stays as it was | `a_sort_comparator_error_propagates_stops_the_calls_and_leaves_the_array`, `an_uncaught_comparator_error_ends_the_program_with_the_callbacks_trace` |
| `sort()` on instances | the left element's `operator<`; no `operator<`, or an array, map or callable element, raises `Invalid operands for < operator`; primitives keep their order | `a_default_sort_of_instances_orders_by_the_languages_less_than` |
| array and map `filter` / `remove_if` predicates | own text | `predicate_and_parallel_body_errors_propagate` |
| `parallel_for` body | own text | `predicate_and_parallel_body_errors_propagate` |
| user `operator+ - * / %`, `< <= > >=`, `==` (and `!=` through it), compound forms on locals, globals and fields | own text | `user_operator_errors_propagate_with_their_own_text` |
| user `operator=` | own text, at every plain store of an object slot | `a_user_operator_assign_error_propagates_with_its_own_text`, `operator_assign_runs_at_every_plain_store` |
| `to_bool()` under `if`, `!`, `?:` and a `bool` slot | travels as the script exception its text would throw (truthiness has no error value) | `to_bool_and_to_string_errors_propagate` |
| `to_string()` under string `+` | own text; the left operand's `to_string()` runs first | `to_bool_and_to_string_errors_propagate` |
| script-declared getter run by `to_json` / `to_binary` | own text or thrown value | `archive_hooks_the_script_declares_propagate_their_errors` |
| script-declared setter and `post_load` run by `from_json` / `from_binary` | own text; a read-only host property and a value a synthesized field store refuses leave the member at its constructed value; a key with no setter is skipped | `archive_hooks_the_script_declares_propagate_their_errors` |
| `extend(obj, Class)` running `post_load` | the hook's error is `extend`'s error | (engine behavior, not pinned by these suites) |
| `reflect::call` / `invoke` / `construct` | the callable's result, error included | (reflection suite) |
| a script function called by the host through its `script_function` | the host receives the `checked_result` | (host API) |

Two call-back families do not propagate into script; both are open questions below (Q5, Q3/Q4):
a script callable converted to a `std::function` (a host API parameter, a signal receiver) reports its
error through `engine::report_script_error` and returns a default, and the membership and string builtins
(`contains`, `index_of`, `has`, `to_string(x)`) never run script at all.

## Known deviations

None. Every derived expectation holds on both backends; the rulings of 2026-09-28 (section 9) settled the last two
(a callee of a top-level block, a typed range-for variable).

## Execution census

Two instruments over the whole Foundry suite on the VM.

**Opcode census** (`x64-Release VMPROF`, `JAISCRIPT_VM_PROFILE`, the per-engine `[vm-profile]` tables summed over
every engine the suite creates). Of 140 dispatch opcodes, 137 are dispatched. The other three:

- `INDEX_ASSIGN` and `INDEX_COMPOUND`: the compiler emits their fused forms; the plain executors run as those forms'
  fallbacks, never from the dispatch switch.
- `YIELD`: executed (the coroutine suites), but the profiler counts an opcode when the next one starts in the same
  dispatch loop, and `YIELD` leaves the loop.

Of 39 cell opcodes, 38 run inside blocks. `C_LAND` runs from the main dispatch loop (`cells_resume`) when a call
made from a block returns, which the block loop's counter does not see: 1782 calls left blocks, 19 of their
landings missed. The least exercised: `T_JLE_FF`, `T_JGE_FF`, `T_JEQ_FF`, `T_JNE_FF` (1 each), `T_MOD_FF` (2),
`FROM_THIS`, `NULL_GUARD`, `T_XOR_II` (5 each); among cell ops `C_BNOT` (8), `C_LNOT` (14), `C_CMPVAL` (21).

**Source coverage** (`x64-Clang-Cov`, `-fprofile-instr-generate -fcoverage-mapping`, llvm-cov):

| File | Lines | Functions | Regions |
|---|---|---|---|
| `vm/vm_compiler.cpp` | 96.8% (5730/5920) | 204/210 | 94.2% |
| `vm/vm_backend.cpp` | 85.2% (13601/15959) | 457/472 | 83.9% |
| `vm/vm_inline.cpp` | 96.0% | 44/44 | 89.0% |
| `vm/region_ir.cpp` | 100% | 6/6 | 97.7% |
| `vm/vm_backend.hpp` | 93.6% | 91/100 | 92.7% |

Compiler lowerings no other test reached, now pinned by Spec Attacks `census_paths`: a ternary store whose arms are
fused binaries (the fused store row, `x = c ? a + b * i : a - i`, including an overflow in an arm), a unary over a
binary landing in a temp (`-(a + b) * 2`, a call argument, a ternary arm), destructured names that live in the
environment (the env-safety scan's destructure rows), and `weak_from_this(1)`'s arity text.

Still unexecuted, by size: the shape lane of `BINARY` and `RETURN_BINARY` (`binary_fast_shape`, 169 lines; the
compiler emits no shape), the boxed chain tail and the map branch of the fused index read, parts of
`enter_constructor_in_loop`, call paths in `exec_call` and `invoke_callee` for opaque callees, a coroutine handle's
`resume` / `done` and a builtin method taken as values (`var f = h.resume;`), `emit_unsupported` (the compiler meets no
unsupported construct), `collect_used_identifiers`, `env_reference_candidate`, `compile_only`, and the non-block branch
of `as_block` (the parser wraps every loop body).

## Open questions

Dev ruled on every open question on 2026-09-28; section 9 pins each ruling on both backends.

**Rows marked contradicted in the ledger.** Registered tests pin the other reading of these claims; each row
names the tests: C-326, D-164, D-221, S-207, S-218, S-283, R33, PD-2, PD-26, PD-40, PD-49, PD-50,
G02-035, G02-038, G03-013b, G03-024a, G03-028, G06-024, G09-178, G09-189, G10-063, G12-002, G13-111, RF-022,
RF-043, GR-087, GR-089, GR-090.

## 2. diagnostics.md, static_checking.md, type_proofs_design.md

Source codes: diag = `docs/diagnostics.md`, sc = `docs/static_checking.md`, tp = `docs/type_proofs_design.md`. "(indirect)" marks a test that runs the construct under the default strict engine and expects success, so the checker's accept verdict is pinned, not a diagnostic text.

| ID | Claim | Source | Pinned by | Fuzzer | Verdict |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| D-1 | Every runtime error text is byte-identical on interpreter and vm | diag intro | semantics/spec_conformance_tests.cpp:family_b_no_member_and_not_a_function_texts; language/review_regression_tests.cpp:callee_first_method_null_receiver_before_args; fuzz/fuzz_smoke_tests.cpp:fixed_seeds_no_new_divergence | yes | pinned |
| D-2 | `catch (e)` sees the text alone (no category suffix) | diag intro | semantics/spec_conformance_tests.cpp:family_d_typed_boundary_texts; semantics/strong_types_tests.cpp:typed_slot_rejection_texts; language/review_regression_tests.cpp:typed_boundary_error_names_dynamic_type | yes | pinned |

## 3. reference_model, namespaces, global_shadowing, EXCEPTION_DESIGN, closures_and_captures, LOCAL_VARIABLES_BEHAVIOR, script_extensible

## 4a. Attack surfaces of the VM fast paths

Each row is a fast path, the assumption it rests on, the event that breaks the assumption, and the tests that stage that event and check the plain-program result. "-" means no test stages it.

| Fast path | Assumption | Breaking event | Test |
|---|---|---|---|
| Cell block | Live-in slots keep their entry kind and stamp | A loop local boxed by a by-reference bind or `[&]` mid-loop | vm/spec_attack_tests.cpp:a_compound_target_is_read_after_its_right_side_inside_a_loop; vm/pilot_cells_tests.cpp:calls_inside_a_block (escape); vm/pilot_cells_tests.cpp:a_statement_increment_lowers_to_the_typed_row_and_the_loop_closes |
| Cell block | Container identity holds until an effect point | A callee rebinds or grows the container | vm/spec_attack_tests.cpp:a_host_callback_writes_the_array_the_loop_is_reading; vm/pilot_cells_tests.cpp:a_landing_sees_what_the_call_changed; vm/pilot_cells_tests.cpp:a_callee_may_reload_arm_operators_or_unwind_through_the_block |
| Cell block | same | A callee shrinks (`pop`/`clear`) the container the loop keeps reading | vm/spec_attack_tests.cpp:a_callee_shrinks_the_array_the_loop_indexes; vm/spec_attack_tests.cpp:a_host_callback_writes_the_array_the_loop_is_reading (host pops the array under a size-headed loop) |
| Cell block | No user code runs between call doors | A class `operator+` on a `var`-array element used in cell arithmetic | vm/spec_attack_tests.cpp:a_class_operator_on_a_var_element_inside_a_loop |
| Cell block | Operator table is fixed | The host registers a numeric operator mid-loop | vm/pilot_cells_tests.cpp:a_callee_may_reload_arm_operators_or_unwind_through_the_block (armed); vm/numeric_operator_transition_tests.cpp:registered_mid_function_before_next_typed_operation |
| Cell block | The budget stop flushes before raising | Budget expires mid-block: are the block's global writes flushed (post-state)? | vm/spec_attack_tests.cpp:a_budget_overrun_inside_a_block_is_terminal_and_leaves_the_flushed_state; vm/pilot_cells_tests.cpp:budget_exhaustion_inside_the_cell_loop_raises_the_same_text (text only); vm/pilot_cluster_tests.cpp:budget_exhaustion_inside_the_loop_raises_the_same_text (text only) |
| Cell block | Debugger not armed | Breakpoint in the body, armed before the call or from a host callback mid-loop | vm/spec_attack_tests.cpp:a_breakpoint_inside_a_closed_loop_fires_every_iteration; vm/spec_attack_tests.cpp:a_breakpoint_armed_by_a_host_callback_mid_loop_fires_before_the_loop_ends |
| Cell block | Record frame | Closed loop in a coroutine body | vm/spec_attack_tests.cpp:a_suspended_loop_reads_what_the_resumer_wrote; vm/pilot_cells_tests.cpp:interleaved_coroutines_each_running_a_closed_loop |
| Cell block | same | A coroutine calls a function that contains a closed loop, and yields between calls | vm/spec_attack_tests.cpp:interleaved_coroutines_calling_a_looped_callee; vm/spec_attack_tests.cpp:a_coroutine_yields_between_calls_to_an_inlinable_leaf |
| Cell block | Flush before unwinding | A callee's throw caught outside the loop | vm/spec_attack_tests.cpp:a_raise_inside_a_closed_typed_loop_is_caught_in_a_caller_with_globals_written; vm/spec_attack_tests.cpp:a_raise_inside_an_inlined_leaf_unwinds_the_loop_with_the_prior_iterations_kept; vm/pilot_cells_tests.cpp:calls_inside_a_block (thrown) |
| Cell block | The callee is probed at every call | The host or a script rebinds the called function mid-loop | vm/spec_attack_tests.cpp:a_leaf_redefined_by_a_host_callback_mid_loop_is_called_with_its_new_body; vm/spec_attack_tests.cpp:a_function_valued_global_rebound_mid_loop; vm/pilot_cells_tests.cpp:stdlib_math_alias_loop_follows_a_rebinding_by_a_call |
| Env cell | Binding and epoch hold between calls | The same unit redeclares a global with a new type or as a property reference | vm/pilot_cells_tests.cpp:a_redeclared_global_after_a_call_lands_by_its_new_type; vm/pilot_cells_tests.cpp:a_global_redeclared_as_a_property_reference_after_compilation_reads_first |
| Env cell / typed row | Environment values are script values | A cpp-bound global (`add_global_ref`) read and written in a loop while a host callback mutates the C++ variable | vm/spec_attack_tests.cpp:a_cpp_bound_global_changes_under_a_loop |
| Cell block | Each call record has its own raw area | The same function recursed into from inside its own closed loop (script or host `as_function()`) | vm/spec_attack_tests.cpp:a_host_callback_re_enters_the_function_running_the_loop |
| Member site | Class shape and accessors are fixed | A callee hot-reloads the class mid-loop (adds `_get_x`, retypes or migrates `x`) | vm/spec_attack_tests.cpp:a_class_reloaded_mid_loop_takes_effect_at_the_member_site (adds `_get_x`; retype and migration unstaged) |
| Member site | The base slot's instance is fixed | A callee with `P& p` replaces `p` or nulls it mid-loop | vm/spec_attack_tests.cpp:a_class_reloaded_mid_loop_takes_effect_at_the_member_site (replace; null unstaged); vm/pilot_cells_tests.cpp:a_member_read_of_a_frame_slot_instance_is_a_block_site (field rewrite) |
| Element alias | The element stays a plain array node | A callee pops or erases the aliased element | vm/spec_attack_tests.cpp:an_element_alias_whose_element_a_callee_removed_raises_the_removed_element_text; vm/pilot_cells_tests.cpp:an_element_alias_declared_in_the_body_is_a_rebind (store, replace, non-array) |
| Range-for block | The iteration's container holds through calls | A callee rebinds the iterated global, or clears it | vm/spec_attack_tests.cpp:a_callee_clears_the_array_a_range_for_walks (clear; rebind unstaged); vm/pilot_cells_tests.cpp:a_range_for_over_an_array_is_a_block (growth) |
| Map-entry container | The key stays present | A callee erases the key mid-loop | vm/pilot_cells_tests.cpp:a_removed_map_entry_refuses_the_block (entry time only) |
| Pinned table | No store site writes the table | A callee stores into the table | vm/pilot_cells_tests.cpp:an_element_reference_is_a_member_containers_base; vm/pilot_cells_tests.cpp:a_table_reached_by_several_member_sites_is_pinned_once |
| Straight-line region | Entered once; a miss at the first instruction goes to the trampoline | Misses, exceptions, parameter kind changes | vm/pilot_cells_tests.cpp:straight_line_regions; vm/pilot_cells_tests.cpp:straight_line_region_kinds_exits_and_errors |
| Straight-line region | Record frame, no debugger | Region inside a coroutine or a parallel worker; breakpoint on a region line | - |
| Graft | Binding still equals the grafted declaration (checked at entry and after every effect point) | A script callee rebinds the leaf name mid-loop | vm/spec_attack_tests.cpp:a_script_function_name_rebound_by_a_callee_mid_loop |
| Graft | same | The host `execute`s a new leaf definition from a native call in the loop | vm/spec_attack_tests.cpp:a_leaf_redefined_by_a_host_callback_mid_loop_is_called_with_its_new_body |
| Graft | same | Reload of the imported unit that declares the leaf | - |
| Graft | Debugger not armed | Breakpoint inside the leaf's body | - |
| Graft | Everything to the left of the call is snapshotted or unwritable by the body | `[&]` captures, `var&`, globals, statics, elements, `this.f` | vm/pilot_cells_tests.cpp:a_graft_that_writes_keeps_captured_and_reference_names_to_its_left; vm/pilot_cells_tests.cpp:an_element_read_ahead_of_a_writing_call_reads_first; vm/pilot_cells_tests.cpp:a_static_member_read_before_a_body_writing_it_keeps_the_call |
| Graft | All arguments are evaluated before the first bind | Binding argument 1 fails after argument 2 had a side effect | vm/spec_attack_tests.cpp:a_graft_binds_no_argument_before_every_argument_is_evaluated |
| Method graft | The receiver's class is at its method epoch | Class reload mid-loop | vm/spec_attack_tests.cpp:a_method_redefined_mid_recursion_resolves_the_new_body (mid-recursion, not mid-loop); vm/pilot_cells_tests.cpp:a_method_graft_follows_reloads (between calls) |
| op_t_* | Operands are canonical same-tag slots | A cpp-bound global as a typed-row operand | vm/spec_attack_tests.cpp:a_cpp_bound_global_changes_under_a_loop |
| op_t_* | Guard misses re-run the original | Shift count ≥64 or negative in a proven loop | vm/spec_attack_tests.cpp:shift_counts_out_of_range_raise_inside_a_counted_loop |
| Speculative lane | Element kind is int/float | Map container, string/bool element, getter reference | vm/pilot_cells_tests.cpp:a_read_through_an_unproven_container_takes_the_typed_row_and_falls_back_the_same; vm/pilot_cells_tests.cpp:a_plain_alias_container_reads_through_the_guard_and_a_shrunken_element_reference_does_not |
| Hoisted reads | Earlier operands cannot run user code after the read | Getter refs, `operator[]`, custom operators, reload into a property reference | vm/pilot_cells_tests.cpp:a_read_that_may_run_user_code_never_runs_ahead_of_an_earlier_operand; vm/pilot_cells_tests.cpp:a_global_redeclared_as_a_property_reference_after_compilation_reads_first |
| MATH_GUARD | Bare `sqrt` is the builtin | A local variable or parameter named `sqrt`/`abs` | vm/spec_attack_tests.cpp:a_local_or_parameter_named_like_a_math_alias_shadows_it |
| MATH_GUARD | Miss between the guard and the op | Region miss | vm/pilot_cells_tests.cpp:stdlib_math_alias_loops_close_as_cell_blocks; vm/pilot_cells_tests.cpp:region_misses_in_a_method_writing_globals_through_aliases |
| Compound in a block | Target is read after the right side (inv §12) | `x += mutate(x)` inside a closed loop | vm/spec_attack_tests.cpp:a_compound_target_is_read_after_its_right_side_inside_a_loop |
| Parallel chunk graft | fn is pure; admission cached per epoch | A helper redefined between regions to write a global | language/parallel_transform_tests.cpp:admission_recheck_after_function_redefine (pure-to-pure only) |
| Parallel borrow | Captured containers are frozen during the region | A transitive helper writes a captured array or map | - |
| Cells in workers | Worker frames are ordinary; a borrow tag is not an array node | Typed counted loop in a transform fn over a captured `array<int>`, with injected misses and an out-of-bounds read | semantics/spec_conformance_tests.cpp:cells_in_a_parallel_worker_match_the_serial_rows (typed counted loop over a captured `array<int>` in a transform fn, injected misses in MISS builds; no out-of-bounds read); language/parallel_transform_tests.cpp:captured_grid_subscript_borrow (var loop, no misses) |
| Parameter elision | The callee's call graph is pure | The parameter used with a user method or accessor, as a range source, or in `same_object` | - |
| Parallel + debugger | Regions are atomic to the debugger | Breakpoint in a body | debug/debugger_tests.cpp:parallel_region_atomic_to_debugger; debug/debugger_tests.cpp:vm_parallel_region_atomic_to_debugger |

## 9. The rulings of 2026-09-28

Source codes: ruling Qn = Dev's ruling on open question n (2026-09-28), gr = `docs/grammar.md`, cc = `docs/closures_and_captures.md`,
diag = `docs/diagnostics.md`, exc = `docs/EXCEPTION_DESIGN.md`, ref = `docs/reference_model.md`, ns = `docs/namespaces.md`,
gs = `docs/global_shadowing.md`, sc = `docs/static_checking.md`, ta = `docs/typed_array_design.md`, embedding 08 = the serialization
chapter, 02/04/05/07/09 = guide chapters. Pins are relative to `Source/JaiScript/source/tests`; `spec:` is
`semantics/spec_conformance_tests.cpp`. Every row runs on the interpreter and the VM, with type proofs on and off.
